# BudgetFlow web — design-system fidelity audit

- **Date:** 2026-11-10. **Branch:** `chore/design-system-audit`, cut from `main` at `b2141aeb`, in the worktree `budget-delegation-and-control-worktrees/audit-design-system`. `main` does not yet contain `a614b2bd` (the dense table on Home, on `fix/visual-rerecord`), so the Home findings describe `main` as it is.
- **Source of truth:** screen 000, `design/preview/html/000-design-system/default.html`, sections 1–13. Per-screen layout comes from `design/stitch/<NNN-slug>/` and intent from `docs/ux/screens/<NNN-slug>.md`. Where an export and the sheet disagree, the sheet governs shared components and the export governs that screen's layout. Conflicts are listed in each area's "Spec conflicts / notes".
- **Scope:** every screen group in `libs/web/features/*`, the shell (`libs/web/shell`), and every shared component in `libs/web/ui/src/lib/*` and `libs/web/ui/src/styles/*`.
- **Audit only:** no product code, `screens.json` or baseline was changed. Everything below lives under `tmp/audit/`, which is gitignored.

## How it was run

| Step            | Command / tool                                                                                                                                                                                          | Result                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| --------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| Isolated stack  | `BF_WEB_PORT=24200 BF_API_PORT=23000 BF_KEYCLOAK_PORT=28080 BF_MAILPIT_PORT=28025 BF_MINIO_PORT=29000 BF_MOCK_PROVIDERS_PORT=24010 BF_PG_PORT=25432 scripts/stack.sh up`, then `scripts/stack.sh reset` | `docker ps`: all 8 `bf-audit-design-system-*` services healthy and bound to these ports. Other stacks hold 4200/3000/8080/5432 and the 3x/4x/5x/6x ranges. Seed settled.                                                             |
| Sign-ins        | `npx playwright test -c apps/web-e2e/playwright.config.ts --project setup`                                                                                                                              | 14 roles. Every role used in `screens.json` has saved sign-in state.                                                                                                                                                                 |
| Capture         | `node scripts/visual/capture.mjs --screens apps/web-e2e/visual/screens.json --out tmp/audit --base http://localhost:24200`                                                                              | 153 screens, 258 states, 774 PNGs (1440 / 834 / 412, light). Exit 1. Findings X9, X10.                                                                                                                                               |
| Design parity   | `node scripts/visual/design-parity.mjs --captures tmp/audit`                                                                                                                                            | 52 pairs, all above the advisory ratio. 31 design states have no capture; 108 captured screens have no design.                                                                                                                       |
| Computed styles | `node tmp/audit/tools/probe.mjs` (written for this audit; replays the `screens.json` states)                                                                                                            | `tmp/audit/probe/*.json`: table header and row heights, per-column header and cell alignment, KPI anatomy, tag icons and tones, bar segment colours, runtime token values.                                                           |
| Inspection      | 8 parallel reviewers, one per area                                                                                                                                                                      | Every capture was opened at all 3 viewports, plus the side-by-sides, the Stitch exports and the source. Every screen was re-captured at full height (see X10). Ad hoc captures are in `tmp/audit/extra*/`, `fullpage-C/`, `tall-D/`. |

**Limits.**

- The MFA page (003) was not reached, because it needs a TOTP code.
- Saved sign-ins expire after about 90 minutes. Late tablet/mobile probes of the AI screens timed out and those screens were checked by eye.
- Severity counts are rows: a shared issue is repeated as a short row on each screen it affects.

| C. Events (043–054b, 053a)                                                   | `findings/C-events.md`                |        9 |      51 |      74 |
| D. Requests, approvals, employee (055–065, 090, 115–121)                     | `findings/D-requests-approvals.md`    |        8 |      73 |      54 |
| E. Rules, AI, insights (066–078, 011a/b, 016b, 017c, 022d, 080f, 113a, 121a) | `findings/E-rules-ai.md`              |       14 |      80 |      76 |
| F. Reports, close, accounting (013–016a, 079–091, 080e, 087\*, 099a)         | `findings/F-reports-close.md`         |        7 |      61 |      73 |
| G. Integrations, channels, mobile, notifications                             | `findings/G-integrations-channels.md` |        2 |      88 |      77 |
| H. Admin, org, finance-admin, extended                                       | `findings/H-admin-extended.md`        |        6 |      50 |      51 |
| **Total**                                                                    |                                       |   **60** | **510** | **495** |

What this means:

- **Budget-safety distinctions fail in shared code, not on individual screens.**
  - Three status tokens the code relies on don't exist, so the colour silently disappears.
  - The bucket bars use their own palette instead of section 10.
  - Status tags show Satori's default icons with tones that differ from section 5.
  - The report table sends labels where the status map expects keys.
- **The prescribed shared anatomy is missing everywhere.**
  - No KPI card has the section 9 anatomy: 237 cards on 54 screens.
  - No table meets section 6: 144 unique tables. None is 52/48 px, none has an owner avatar, none has a frozen/zero stripe, and only 2 use `satTableWithRowStates`.
  - Numeric header and cell alignment is consistent at 1440 (0 mismatches), but 11 numeric columns are left-aligned, and at 834/412 key columns are pushed off-screen.
- **Visual QA has never seen most of the product.**
  - Every capture is cut at one viewport height.
  - The overflow check can't see inside the shell's scroller.
  - About 70 routes and states, including Home and 11 approved Stitch exports, are not in `screens.json`.

Area-local recurring IDs are scoped to their own section: B1–B9 (budgets), C1–C9 (events; renamed from the reviewer's E1–E9), E1–E11 (rules/AI), and G11–G17 (integrations; renamed from the reviewer's S11–S17). S1–S10 are the shared IDs written up in area A.

# Findings by area, screen and component

Each area section groups rows under `### <screen-id or component>`. Columns: viewport · what is wrong · design-system reference · severity · concrete fix.

## Area A — shell, auth, Home, email action and shared UI components

Branch `chore/design-system-audit` (from `main` b2141aeb). Stack on web :24200 with the deterministic seed. Evidence:
captures `tmp/audit/screens/`, parity `tmp/audit/design-parity/`, probe JSON `tmp/audit/probe/`, extra captures
(Home as CEO and department head, Keycloak sign-in, rendered approval email) `tmp/audit/extra/`, nav coverage
`tmp/audit/extra/nav-coverage.json`. Viewports: desktop 1440, tablet 834, mobile 412, light theme.

Note: `main` does not contain commit a614b2bd ("dense data table on Home", `libs/web/ui/src/styles/data-table.scss`,
on `fix/visual-rerecord`). Findings describe `main` as it is; re-check S2 after that branch merges.

### Not covered by visual QA

- **011-executive-dashboard (Home `/`)** — 4 Stitch states, no screens.json entry (major).
- **000a `search-open`** — Stitch state, not implemented, no capture. **000a `user-menu-open`** — the capture is named `account-menu-open`, so design-parity never pairs it with the Stitch file (rename the state).
- **000a** session-expired (`sat-401`), shell load error (`sat-500`), loading ghosts, not-member page, `/forbidden` route — no captures.
- **001-sign-in, 003-mfa-verification** — Stitch exports exist; Keycloak pages never captured.
- **061a** — only `link-invalid`; valid preview, reject form, result and held/used states, plus the rendered email.
- **Discussion panel** (shared `bf-discussion-panel`) — no state opens it.
- **Nav pages not in screens.json** (from `tmp/audit/extra/nav-coverage.json`, owners outside fork A): `/budgets/requests`, `/org`, `/org/people`, `/org/headcount`, `/org/cost-centers`, `/org/import`, `/finance/actuals` (Stitch 080a/080b), `/finance/gl-tie-out` (Stitch 080c), `/finance/periods` (Stitch 108), `/finance/doa`, `/finance/rates` (Stitch 107), `/finance/legal-entities`, `/finance/gl`, `/finance/reason-codes`, `/finance/vendors`.
- **screens.json config gaps**: expected 403/404/409/422 responses not in `allowRequests` (016a forbidden-owner, 087a commentary-required, 061f turned-off, 102d widget-removed) make the capture exit 1.

### Spec conflicts / notes

- **Fonts**: the sheet uses Plus Jakarta Sans (headlines) and Noto Sans via Google Fonts, Tailwind hex and Material Icons; Satori ships Figtree + Noto Sans and the harness bans `font-family`, raw colours and Material Symbols. Treat the sheet's hex as documentation of Satori tokens (they match exactly except accrued) and keep Figtree; only weight (S7) is fixable.
- **Error tone**: the sheet's error-container (`#ffdad9`) for Exhausted and the hard-block banner has no Satori equivalent — `sat-banner` variants are info/warning/important/success and `sat-status-tag` tones add important-loud. Use `important` (banner) and `important-loud` (Exhausted tag).
- **Accrued** `#5d5c72` has no Satori token; `--mat-sys-secondary` is `#5c5b7f` (closest).
- **Icons**: sheet icon names (edit_note, pie_chart, donut_large, verified, exposure_zero, event_busy, folder_off, history) are Material Icons and not in the Satori sprite; the nearest Satori names are listed under S3.
- **Platform nav logo** "Content Innovation Cloud" is built into `sat-platform-nav`; the Stitch "BudgetFlow ENTERPRISE" logo needs a Satori input or acceptance.
- **Primary financial action**: frontend-angular says it uses `satImportantButton`; the sheet (§4, §11) uses filled primary for Allocate/Approve and error fill only for Set to zero. `decision-form` follows the sheet (Approve flat primary, Reject important). Align the rule wording.
- **061a**: the Stitch export depicts the email in a mail client; the `/email-action` landing page has no approved design.
- **Keycloak theme** cannot consume `sat.theme`; its hex `:root` block needs a documented exception and a generated source.
- **Available text colour**: sheet uses success `#4d9c4c` for text; recommend `--sat-sys-on-success-container` for contrast (accessibility checks are deferred, so this is advisory).

## Area B — Budgets screens (019–032a, 089, 031a, 022c, 023a)

Scope: `libs/web/features/budgets` (+ `libs/web/features/extended` scenario pages for 031a). Branch `chore/design-system-audit` (from `main`, b2141aeb), stack on :24200, deterministic seed.

Evidence: captures `tmp/audit/screens/` (99 PNGs), full-length re-captures `tmp/audit/extra/*--tall.png` (the shell's inner scroll container cuts every `fullPage` capture at one viewport — see Notes), ad-hoc captures `tmp/audit/extra/` (023a, 022c, controls dialog, /budgets/requests), dialog computed styles `tmp/audit/B-dialogs-desktop.txt` / `B-dialogs-mobile.txt`, probe JSON `tmp/audit/probe/`.

Shared IDs used below (detailed write-up owned by fork A): **S1** KPI card ≠ section 9 · **S2** tables ≠ section 6 dense table (52/48 px, uppercase 600 headers on surface-container-low, sat-avatar owner, code chip, frozen/zero left stripe) · **S3** status-tag tones/icons ≠ section 5 (frozen shows Satori `critical` icon, not lock; set to zero shows `error`, not zero) · **S4** undefined `--sat-sys-{info,success,warning}` tokens · **S5** budget bar colours ≠ section 10 · **S6** money text outside `bf-money` · **S7** page/dialog titles Figtree 400 instead of 600 · **S8** cards 16 px (`corner-large`) instead of 12 px (`corner-medium`) · **S9** visual-QA coverage gaps · **S10** inline `font-family`.

Budgets-wide findings first proposed here (cite as **B1–B9**):

- **B1** Financial dialogs never use `satImportantButton` (0 uses in the budgets lib); set to zero paints a custom `.bf-danger-button` (`dialogs.scss:219-222`); every dialog is its own component — there is no shared confirm-with-reason dialog (frontend-angular "Financial actions"). The extended lib's scenario promote already does it right (`scenario-editor.page.ts:370-371`).
- **B2** `mat-dialog-title` is put on the flex header wrapper (`control-dialogs.ts:71,286,533`, `money-dialogs.ts` same pattern), so the 40 px icon tile sits ~5 px under the top edge, clipped by the 28 px corner, and the header scrolls away with the content.
- **B3** Amount fields never enter the Material error state (`mat-form-field-invalid`: false and no `mat-error` in all 7 blocked/validation states measured); the reason is shown only in a banner, often below the fold.
- **B4** Hand-rolled tabs: `.bf-tab` buttons (`list.page.ts:56-84,149-162`) and `.bf-detail__tab` (`detail.page.html:147-167`) instead of Satori navigation tabs; they wrap onto 2–3 lines at 834/412 px.
- **B5** No `MatSnackBar` anywhere in the budgets lib; success is a `sat-banner variant="success"` (`detail.page.html:121-125`, plans/templates/per-head) — section 11 toast + frontend-angular "Success: snackbar, focus returned".
- **B6** Option cards hand-rolled as buttons with no radio indicator: `.bf-destination` (`dialogs.scss:115-144`, `control-dialogs.ts:371-400`, increase dialog funded-from) — use `mat-radio-group` with card styling on `bf-*` classes.
- **B7** Impact previews computed in the component: `money-dialogs.ts:131,140,322,331`, `control-dialogs.ts:364`, `change-requests.ts:273` (frontend-angular: "never compute balances … impact preview the API returns").
- **B8** No responsive table strategy: at 834 and 412 px the key money column (Available / Amount) and status sit off-screen inside `.bf-table-scroll`, no sticky first column; rows wrap to 80–100 px.
- **B9** Money inputs have no `matTextPrefix` currency and no grouping (`2500`, `999999`) — section 7 amount field shows `USD 5,000.00` with an internal prefix.

### Spec conflicts / notes

- Every Stitch export in this scope is marked "proposed - awaiting user review" in its `meta.json` / ux spec, not approved. 022c's spec says to build from the spec, not the image (no skip-error-rows checkbox).
- The Stitch exports render money in a monospace face (`USD 10,000.00` in JetBrains-Mono style) and headings in Plus Jakarta Sans 700; §2 and Satori say Noto Sans tabular numbers and Satori's own headline font (Figtree). The design system wins: keep `bf-money` (tabular Noto) and do not declare `font-family`; only the weight (600) is adoptable.
- `sat-banner` has no `error` variant and a fixed icon (`info | circle_check | critical | error`), so §8 "Hard block" (error container + block icon) can only be `variant="important"` + `border`; set-to-zero (also important) and hard block then share a tone. Distinguish with the "Blocked:" lead-in and a disabled action that says why; do not override `.sat-banner` internals.
- §10 Accrued `#5d5c72` has no Satori token; the nearest is `--mat-sys-secondary` (#5c5b7f), which the spectrum currently uses for _pending_ — use it for accrued and move pending to `--sat-sys-info-icon`.
- §11 dialog surface is white in the sheet; Satori renders `surface-container-low` (#f4f3f7). Leave Satori's value or change only via `mat.dialog-overrides((container-color: var(--mat-sys-surface-container-lowest)))`.
- 031 and 032 screens are mapped in screens.json to tabs of the budget detail page that are different screens; their baselines therefore "pass" against designs they do not implement.
- Shared `tall.mjs` under `tmp/audit/tools/` was overwritten by another fork mid-run; fork B's tools are `B-dialog.mjs`, `B-adhoc.mjs`, `B-auth/` (fresh sign-ins in `tmp/audit/B-auth/` because the shared `tmp/e2e-auth` Keycloak sessions expired after ~90 min — other forks may hit "Sign in to your account" captures).

## Area C — Events, co-funding and recharges (043–054b, 053a)

Scope: `libs/web/features/events` (routes `/events/**`, `/finance/recharges`; `/contributions/:id` is fork D's 056a).
Branch `chore/design-system-audit` from `main` (b2141aeb), stack on :24200, seed reset, role per `screens.json`.

Evidence (all under `tmp/audit/`):

- Viewport captures `screens/` and parity `design-parity/` (from `capture.mjs`/`design-parity.mjs`).
- **Full-length** shots `fullpage-C/` (`tools/fullpage-C.mjs`): the standard captures stop at the viewport, see C9.
- Extra uncovered routes/states `fullpage-C/extra/` + `probe-C-extra/` (`tools/extra-C.json`).
- Computed-style probe `probe/<id>--<state>--desktop.json`; inner-scroller overflow `overflow-C.txt` (`tools/overflow-C.mjs`).
- Mobile clipping proof `fullpage-C/clip-054b-funding-mobile.png`, `fullpage-C/clip-047e-split-mobile.png`.

Shared IDs used below: **S1** KPI card ≠ section 9 · **S2** table ≠ section 6 (52/48 px, uppercase 600 header on
surface-container-low, avatar, code chip, state stripe, paginator) · **S3** status tag tone/icon ≠ section 5 ·
**S4** undefined `--sat-sys-{info,success,warning}` · **S5** budget-bar colours ≠ section 10 · **S6** money as text
outside `bf-money` · **S7** page title / no `sat-header-bar` · **S8** card radius 16 px vs 12 px · **S9** visual-QA
coverage · **S10** inline font-family. Events-local shared items **C1–C9** are defined at the end; rows cite them.

Probe facts common to every events screen (desktop): h1 Figtree 24/400; `.bf-kpi` label 12/500 no uppercase,
value 22/400, radius 16 px, 0 icons, 0 footers; mat-table header 56 px / rows 52 px / header 14/500 on white;
`bf-split-table` header 37 px / rows 43–61 px / header 12/500 transparent; 0 tables with `sat-avatar`; 0 sortable.

### Events-local shared items (resolve many rows at once)

- **C1 — approved/funded fills invisible.** `libs/web/ui/src/styles/page-layout.scss:256` `.bf-progress__fill--approved { background: var(--sat-sys-success) }` is undefined. Used at `events-list.page.ts:147` (fully funded), `events-dashboard.page.ts:187` (RSVP), `cofunding-board.page.ts:128,201`, and event-detail KPI at 100 %. Use `var(--sat-sys-success-icon)`; ideally replace all funding bars with one shared section-10 bar component (S5).
- **C2 — captions styled as empty states.** `<caption class="bf-empty">` at `event-people.page.ts:541`, `event-feedback.page.ts:141`, `event-quotes.page.ts:127`, `event-scorecard.page.ts:187,223,277`: centred with empty-state padding. Add `.bf-table-caption { caption-side: top; text-align: start; padding: 0 20px 8px; font: var(--mat-sys-body-small); color: var(--mat-sys-on-surface-variant); }` in `page-layout.scss`.
- **C3 — tables clipped on mobile.** `bf-split-table` placed in an `overflow: hidden` `.bf-card` without `.bf-table-scroll`: `event-feedback.page.ts:140`, `event-people.page.ts:540`, `event-scorecard.page.ts:186,222,276`.
- **C4 — no mobile layout for tables.** 11 events screens scroll tables sideways at 412 px (`overflow-C.txt`); specs ask for cards. One responsive pattern in the shared dense-table style (stacked label/value rows below 600 px) fixes all.
- **C5 — two navigation models for one event.** Overview tabs + section tabs in one row (`event-detail.page.html:278-336`) vs `event-tabs.ts` on sub-pages. Breadcrumbs stop at "Home › Event" everywhere (no Events level, no event name).
- **C6 — red important button for non-financial actions.** New template / Save template (`event-templates.page.ts:63,225`), Send invitations (`event-people.page.ts:161`), Send the survey now (`event-feedback.page.ts:204`).
- **C7 — `bf-chip` as status or tag.** 28 uses in events: Overdue, Severe, Expired, Home budget, Earmark, N pending, Live, tab counters. Map them to `sat-status-tag` (state), `sat-category-tag` (category), `satInlineChip` (code or count).
- **C8 — raw enum values in copy.** Event type, categories, needs, mode, vendor status, audit summaries. Add one label map per enum in `libs/web/data-access` or return labels from the API.
- **C9 — capture harness misses below-the-fold and in-scroller overflow.** Satori's `main.sat-platform-nav-main-content` is the scroller, so `capture.mjs` `fullPage: true` captures exactly the viewport (every events capture is 1440×900 / 834×1112 / 412×915). Its overflow check reads `document.documentElement` only, so C3/C4 never fire. Fix `scripts/visual/capture.mjs` to expand or scroll that container and to measure overflow inside it (S9).

### Spec conflicts / notes

- **Hard-block tone.** `sat-banner` offers only info / warning / important / success (types d.ts), so a hard block cannot use the error container from sections 5 and 8. Use `important` for hard blocks and `warning` for soft warnings. The tones stay distinct; update section 8.
- **Red buttons.** Section 4 keeps red for destructive actions only, but frontend-angular requires `satImportantButton` (red) for the primary financial action, e.g. "Apply new split", "Close and return surplus", "Submit for approval". Decide which rule wins; this audit followed frontend-angular for financial actions and flagged only non-financial uses (C6).
- **Lifecycle steps.** The 046/050 Stitch exports show Funding / Committed / Reconciled steps, while domain-invariants lists Draft, Submitted, Approved, Funded, In progress, Completed, Reconciling, Closed. The implementation follows the domain; the exports should be corrected.
- **Fonts.** The exports and section 2 use Plus Jakarta Sans 600/700 headings, but the app renders Satori's Figtree 400. Font family can't change (no `font-family` rule); weight could use a token if Satori exposes one (S7).
- **Accrued colour.** Section 10's Accrued #5d5c72 has no Satori token; the closest is `--mat-sys-secondary` (#5c5b7f).
- **Visual baselines.** 046a "Live · updated …", activity timestamps and the 047e success banner use the real clock and aren't masked, so baselines can drift.
- **Out of scope.** `/contributions/:id` (056a) is fork D's. Its live-activity term defect is the same as 046a's, sourced from `approvals.service.ts:2061`.

## Area D — Requests, approvals, employee (055–065, 090, 115–121)

Scope: libs/web/features/requests, libs/web/features/approvals, libs/web/features/employee, plus 056a
(its page lives in libs/web/features/events/src/lib/contribution.page.ts) and the shared libs/web/ui pieces
these screens are built from (decision-form.ts, budget-impact.ts, approval-route.ts).
Stack: worktree stack on :24200, deterministic seed, light theme. Evidence used:

- captures tmp/audit/screens/<id>--<state>--<vp>--light.png (all clipped to viewport height, see S9-D below),
- tall re-captures tmp/audit/tall-D/<id>--<state>--<vp>.png (same screens.json states, viewport grown to the inner scroller),
- extra states not in screens.json tmp/audit/extra-D/\*.png (filled request forms, hard block, delegations),
- design-parity side-by-sides tmp/audit/design-parity/, Stitch exports design/stitch/<id>/, specs docs/ux/screens/<id>.md,
- computed-style probe tmp/audit/probe/<id>--<state>--{desktop,tablet,mobile}.json (summary tmp/audit/d-probe.txt).

Shared IDs used below (fork D's working mapping; fork A owns the detailed write-up — reconcile names there):
S1 KPI card ≠ section 9 · S2 table ≠ section 6 · S3 status tag icons/tones ≠ section 5 · S4 undefined tokens
`--sat-sys-{info,success,warning}` · S5 budget bar colours ≠ section 10 · S6 money outside `bf-money` · S7 page-title
typography · S8 card radius 16px vs 12px · S9 visual-QA coverage.

Where S4 shows in this scope: `.bf-amount--pending/--approved` (page-layout.scss:423,427) are **not** used by
requests/approvals/employee (only events: event-detail.page.html:248,402, cofunding-board.page.ts:169,178,248).
Here S4 hits through `libs/web/ui/src/lib/budget-impact.ts:190` (This request value untoned), `:222` (pending segment
and its legend dot transparent) and `libs/web/ui/src/lib/decision-form.ts:229` (passed-check icons not success-toned).

### Spec conflicts / notes

- Hard block vs set to zero: §8 asks for an error-container hard block with a "block" icon, §5 gives Set to zero the important (rose) tone. `sat-banner` only offers info / warning / important / success and a fixed icon per variant (types: `icon: Signal<"info"|"circle_check"|"critical"|"error">`), so a hard block can only be `important` — the same tone as set to zero. Overriding the banner internals would break `no-ds-internals-overrides`; keep `important` and rely on copy + disabled action, or ask design to accept that mapping.
- §6 left state stripe has no Satori row state (`satRowState` supports selected/active only). Not triggered in this scope (no frozen/zero rows in these tables); 055's "On hold: budget frozen" row would need the `bf-row--frozen` class from S2.
- Typography: the sheet uses Plus Jakarta Sans/Noto Sans; Satori renders Figtree/Noto Sans and the harness forbids `font-family`. Weight (600 for headlines) is achievable, family is not (S7).
- 056 Stitch is a request hub; the build routes by `?kind=`. Either adopt the hub (recommended, design governs layout) or update docs/ux/screens/056 to describe the kind-specific form.
- Stitch 059/060/061 put Approve / Reject in a header action bar and a dialog; the build's inline `bf-decision-form` also conflicts with frontend-angular's "shared confirm-with-reason dialog" rule — two sources agree, so this is a fix, not a conflict.
- Stitch 058 shows invented policy codes ("POL-FIN-08"); spec says ignore them.
- 056a ref format: build "IDC-00002", Stitch "IDBCR-2026-0031" — data format, not a design token issue.
- `bf-load-state` text for 056a reads "We could not load the contribution request" — fix together with the exact-term rows.
- Tooling note: tmp/audit/tools/tall.mjs was overwritten by fork D at 12:26 with an equivalent generic version (same args: --only, --viewports, --out); fork D then used tall-D.mjs. If another fork depended on the earlier tall.mjs behaviour, re-check its output.

## Area E — Rules and policies, AI and insights screens

Branch `chore/design-system-audit` (from `main` b2141aeb). Stack: this worktree's own stack on web :24200, deterministic seed, frozen clock.
Libraries: `libs/web/features/rules`, `libs/web/features/ai`, `libs/web/features/insights` (only `audit-log.page.ts`, screen 088, which is audited by the reports/audit fork). Screen 011b lives in `libs/web/features/extended` and is reported here because it was in this fork's scope.

How this was checked

- The shared captures in `tmp/audit/screens/` are cut off at the viewport (1440×900, 834×1112, 412×915). The shell scrolls an inner container, so `fullPage` never grows. Every screen and state here was captured again at full height with `tmp/audit/tools/fullcap-e.mjs` into `tmp/audit/extra/full/`, with tablet and mobile tiled side by side in `tmp/audit/extra/tiles/`. I opened every image.
- Computed styles come from `tmp/audit/probe/*--desktop.json`, plus tablet and mobile for 066–078. The tablet/mobile probe timed out on every AI screen: their roles' saved sign-ins had probably expired and I did not re-sign-in over the shared auth files. Alignment on those screens was checked by eye in the full-height captures.
- Only one screen in scope has a Stitch export: `design/stitch/069-rule-builder/`. It was compared against the 068 capture with `design-parity.mjs` → `tmp/audit/extra/parity-069/design-parity/`. Every other screen was judged against the design system sheet (000) and its `docs/ux/screens/*.md` spec.
- Shared IDs S1–S10 are owned and written up by fork A. Rows below only note where they visibly apply. E1–E11 are issues that repeat within this area.

### Spec conflicts / notes

- 000 §2 names Plus Jakarta Sans for headlines and §6 uses a mono font for currency codes. Satori loads Figtree / Noto Sans and the rules forbid `font-family`, so the font choice follows Satori. Only the weight (600) can be matched, through a token (S7).
- 000 §5 shows Exhausted in the error container; Satori tags have no error tone. The closest is `important-loud`. Same for the §8 hard block used in E6.
- 000 §10 Accrued #5d5c72 has no Satori token. `--mat-sys-secondary` (#5c5b7f) is the nearest (fork A, S5).
- No rows were found where a numeric header and its cells disagree (probe at desktop for all, tablet/mobile for 066–078). The only alignment defect is 069's left-aligned "This request" money column (both header and cells).
- Out of my scope, but the same E3 defect (`satImportantButton` without `mat-flat-button`) exists in `close/close-dialogs.ts:205`, `close/period.page.html:33`, `close/year-end-dialogs.ts:239`, `close/year-end-run.page.html:35`. On a stroked host (important colour but not filled): `approvals/email-action.page.ts:125`, `events/contribution.page.ts:228`, `events/event-detail.page.html:203`, `mobile/admin-channels.page.ts:201,245`, `mobile/chat-cards.page.ts:217,253`, `mobile/mobile-approvals.page.ts:358`, `ui/decision-form.ts:122`. Worth a shared row.
- The tablet/mobile probe timed out on every AI-route state (roles other than finance-admin), most likely because the saved sign-ins under `tmp/e2e-auth/` expired. Rerun the Playwright `setup` project before any further probe run.

## Area F — reports, close, accounting (design-system audit)

Scope: screens 079, 013, 080–086, 015a, 015b, 016a, 087, 088, 091, 080e (3 screen ids), 087a, 087b, 087b-run, 087c, 099a
(all states), plus the uncaptured accounting routes (080a, 080b, 080c with Stitch exports; 080d, 085a, matched,
mapping rules, event reconciliation). Libraries: `libs/web/features/reports`, `close`, `accounting` (088 lives in
`insights`, 099a in `integrations`).

Evidence: viewport captures `tmp/audit/screens/`; full-length desktop shots `tmp/audit/extra/tall/` (the standard
captures stop at the viewport, see Notes N3); accounting captures + parity `tmp/audit/extra/acct/`; probe JSON
`tmp/audit/probe/` (desktop) and `tmp/audit/extra/probe-F/` (tablet, mobile); KPI overflow measurement
`tmp/audit/tools/kpi-overflow.mjs`. Stack seeded on :24200. No product code changed.

Shared IDs (detail owned by fork A): S1 KPI card (§9) · S2 dense table (§6) · S3 status-tag tones/icons (§5) ·
S4 undefined `--sat-sys-{info,success,warning}` · S5 budget bar colours (§10) · S6 money outside `bf-money` ·
S7 page title/header typography · S8 card radius 16 vs 12 · S9 visual-QA coverage · S10 inline font-family.
New candidates found here are N1–N11 under "Spec conflicts / notes"; rows cite them.

Severity: **blocking** = budget-safety pair rendered identically/invisibly, or a money figure shown wrongly;
**major** = wrong or missing prescribed component/anatomy, broken layout at a target viewport, missing state;
**minor** = polish, copy, consistency.

---

### Shared to these libraries — `bf-report-table` / `bf-report-chart` (`reports/src/lib/report-frame.ts`)

Used by 080–087, 015a, 015b and the board pack 016a, so each fix here resolves 11 screens.

| all            | Status cells render through `bf-budget-status` with the API's _label_ (`report-frame.ts:274`); the map is keyed by enum (`set_to_zero`), so every label misses and falls back to neutral: "Set to zero", "Frozen", "completed", "in_progress", "encumbered", "earmarked", "open" all show as the same grey tag with a circle-minus icon (016a, 085). N4.                                                                                                         |
| all            | Row tags (`showRowTags`) toned by `tagStatus()` = `info` for anything except "Commentary due" (`report-frame.ts:372-374`): "Set to zero" and "Frozen" both info with the same `critical` icon on 087.                                                                                                                                                                                                | §5; budget-safety pair                                                                                                              | blocking | Map row tags by key: frozen → `bf-budget-status` (info + lock icon), set_to_zero → important; S3.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| all            | Chart fill uses `var(--categorical-data-viz-1, var(--mat-sys-primary))` (`:486`) but the dataviz token sheet is never imported, so every series is primary violet: Authorized, Actual, Forecast and Available bars look the same.                                                                                                                                                                    | §13 Satori chart palette (violet/teal/blue/amber via data-viz tokens)                                                               | major    | Import `@hylandsoftware/satori-tokens/css/dataviz` once in `apps/web/src/styles.scss` (allowed for charts) and give each series its own `--categorical-data-viz-N`. N1.                                                                                                                                                                                         |

Actual vs planned reads as equal.                                                                                                                                              | §13 (one shared axis, grouped bars); budget-safety "actual vs planned"                                                              | major    | Scale all series of one chart to a shared max, or draw grouped/stacked bars per row (one row per budget, series side by side). N2.                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |

Visual-QA config gaps (minor): `016a forbidden-owner` (403), `087a commentary-required` (422) need `allowRequests`; capture
`fullPage: true` is ineffective (N3), so every state's below-the-fold content is unchecked by `/visual-qa`.

### Spec conflicts / notes

- **082 Stitch + spec vs §10.** The 082 export/spec colour Actual violet, Approved commitments teal, Pending hatched
  amber, Available grey. §10 says Actual `#006b61` (tertiary teal), Encumbered `#967c39` (warning amber),
  Pre-encumbered `#5384f1` (info blue), Available `#4d9c4c` (success green). The system governs shared components, so
  the shared `bf-budget-bar` (S5) should follow §10; the 082 layout (stacked per region, legend, View as table) stays.
- **Forecast treatment.** §10 prescribes a dashed outline; the build hatches forecast cells/KPIs (087, 087a). Either is
  distinct, but it must be one treatment on every screen (086 and the 087 KPI currently have none).
- **Typography.** §2 headlines Plus Jakarta Sans 600; Satori renders Figtree 400 (`--mat-sys-headline-small`). Font
  family is Satori's to decide (no `font-family` allowed); the weight is S7.
- **Section 11 drawer.** Specs 016a, 079, 082, 088 call for a side sheet; every drill-down/detail here is inline.
- **Spec 087 references `libs/web/ui/charts`**, which does not exist; charts live in `report-frame.ts`.
- New cross-cutting candidates for the parent's shared list:
  - **N1** dataviz tokens never imported (`grep satori-tokens/css/dataviz` = 0): every `--categorical-data-viz-*`
    falls back.
  - **N2** `bf-report-chart` scales each series separately.
  - **N3** captures are viewport-sized (1440×900, 834×1112, 412×915) because the shell scrolls an inner container;
    `fullPage` captures nothing below the fold. Fix in `capture.mjs` (unlock the shell scroll container before the
    screenshot, as `tmp/audit/tools/tallshot.mjs` does).
  - **N4** report status cells pass labels to key-based status components.
  - **N5** `satImportantButton` without `mat-flat-button` (4 places in close).
  - **N6** `bf-load-state` duplicated 17×.
  - **N7** native date inputs in report/year-end filters.
  - **N8** KPI values overflow at the 180px `minmax` (`page-layout.scss:124`) because `bf-money` is `nowrap`
    (`money.ts:29-30`): up to +21px on 5 screens at 1440 and +18px at 412.
  - **N9** `.bf-filters` bottom padding 0: last field touches the card border on mobile.
  - **N10** dates: raw ISO in copy, mixed `MMM d, y` vs `d MMM y`, date-only values shifted by timezone.
  - **N11** `sat-empty-state` used without illustration/action inside cards and clipped.
- Data notes (not design): the board pack for closed Sep 2026 shows USD 0.00 everywhere with "No snapshot" — the
  closed period has no snapshot in the seed; the 087a closed-cycle KPI matches 087 default.

## Area G — integrations, channels, mobile, notifications, marketplace, embed

Scope: 29 screen ids, 50 states x 3 viewports (150 captures). Libraries: `libs/web/features/integrations` (hub, connector frame, sync, errors, pushes, API keys, webhooks, identity, ecosystem/_) and `libs/web/features/mobile` (m/_, notifications, me/\*, admin/channels, admin/digests, link/chat). None of these screens has a Stitch export, so section 000 plus the UX spec (`docs/ux/screens/<id>.md`) govern; there are no design-parity images for this group.

Evidence: official captures `tmp/audit/screens/`; full-height re-shots `tmp/audit/full-g/` (see note N1: the official full-page captures stop at the viewport height); probe JSON `tmp/audit/probe/*--{desktop,tablet,mobile}.json`; ad-hoc probes `tmp/audit/tools/g-*.mjs` (empty-state slots, mobile fonts/tab bar, hidden table columns).

Shared ids (detail owned by fork A): S1 KPI card anatomy (sec. 9) · S2 dense table (sec. 6) · S3 status tag icon/tone (sec. 5) · S4 undefined `--sat-sys-{info,success,warning}` · S5 budget bar colours (sec. 10) · S6 money in prose outside `bf-money` · S7 heading weight/family · S8 card radius 16 vs 12 · S9 visual-QA coverage · S10 inline font-family.
Proposed new cross-cutting ids from this fork (for the parent to merge):

- **G11** `sat-empty-state` / `sat-*-error-state` drop every child without a slot directive — actions need `satEmptyStateAction` / `satErrorStateAction`, illustrations `satEmptyStateIllustration`. App-wide: 41 projected actions are silently removed, including the "Try again" button in all 17 per-feature `load-state.ts:29` copies (every 500 state has no retry). Integrations 7, mobile 6.
- **G12** Visual-QA tooling: full-page captures are cut at the viewport height (the shell scrolls an inner container), and the overflow check only measures the document, so tables clipped inside `.bf-table-scroll` never fail.
- **G13** `satImportantButton` (rose) is used for routine create/connect actions as well as for destructive confirmations (~25 uses in these two libs), so "Connect a system" looks the same as "Revoke this key".
- **G14** Raw enum values in UI copy and tags (`approved`, `waiting`, `active`, `slack`, `approval.assigned`, `finance-admin`, `approvals`).
- **G15** Hand-rolled `.bf-tabs` link and button bars instead of `sat-navigation-tabs` (integrations lib: 6 places); they wrap to 2–3 lines at 834 and 412 px.
- **G16** `sat-header-bar` is prescribed by every spec here but never used (page headers are `.bf-page__header`).
- **G17** No responsive table pattern: at 412 px, 23 states push Status and/or Actions columns (Revoke, Remove, Unlink) out of view inside a horizontally scrolled container with no affordance; 10 tablet states clip Actions.

Severity: blocking = breaks a required state, budget-safety rule or the visual-QA gate; major = clear deviation from a prescribed component or spec, or loss of information or affordance; minor = polish.

### Spec conflicts / notes

- **N1 — capture height (G12).** Every "full page" capture in `tmp/audit/screens/` is exactly the viewport height (900 / 1112 / 915 px) because the shell scrolls an inner container. Below-the-fold content (most of 092, 092a–c, 059c, 061e, 101-webhooks, 102d) has never been in a baseline. My re-shots unlock the scroller (`tmp/audit/tools/fullshot.mjs`). Fix in `scripts/visual/capture.mjs` and `apps/web-e2e/src/visual/visual.spec.ts`: before the screenshot, expand the shell's scroll container (or screenshot `main` with `scrollIntoView` stitching). Artefact in the re-shots: after unlocking, the mobile nav drawer's icons ghost along the left edge and the rail stops at the viewport height — ignore those.
- **N2 — overflow check (G12/G17).** `capture.mjs` measures `documentElement.scrollWidth`, so tables clipped inside `.bf-table-scroll` never fail (0 overflow findings while 23 mobile states hide columns). Add a check that compares `table.scrollWidth` with its scrolling ancestor and reports hidden header cells (logic in `tmp/audit/tools/g-hscroll.mjs`).
- **N3 — G11 is app-wide.** Found while auditing 099; the counts per library are in `tmp/audit/tools/g-empty.mjs` output and the scan in this fork's transcript (41 dropped actions; every `load-state.ts:29` retry). The shell's 403 state (`sat-403-error-state` with illustration and action) is correct and is the pattern to copy.
- **Spec 092 vs sec. 4 / harness:** spec 092 (and 061e, 092d, 102d) prescribe `satImportantButton` for Connect; section 4 and `frontend-angular` reserve the important button for the primary _financial_ action, with a primary filled pill for other primary actions. The system governs shared components, so I recommend `mat-flat-button`; the specs need updating.
- **Spec 101 `satInlineChip` (scopes, events) vs Satori API:** `integrations.scss:28` notes Satori's inline chip is for buttons and links only, so static scopes use a hand-rolled `.bf-chip`. Recommend `sat-category-tag category="gray"` for static labels and updating spec 101.
- **Sec. 8 hard-block colour vs Satori:** section 8 shows the hard block on `error-container`; `sat-banner` only offers `info | warning | important | success`, so `important` is the correct Satori hard-block banner and nothing should override it (`no-ds-internals-overrides`).
- **Sec. 12 empty-state typography vs Satori:** section 12 shows a 16 px bold headline and 12 px description; `sat-empty-state` renders 28 px / 400 and 16 px. Satori governs its internals; only the surrounding padding (`.bf-card__body`) and the slot content are ours to fix.
- **Toggles vs tabs:** the mobile library already uses `sat-navigation-tabs` (notifications, admin channels, digests, chat cards); integrations uses six hand-rolled bars (`integrations-nav.ts:9`, `ecosystem-nav.ts:12`, `connection.page.html:135`, `errors.page.html:23`, `pushes.page.ts:65`, `duplicates.page.html:13`), so the app shows two tab looks side by side.
- **Per-library copies:** `load-state.ts` exists in every feature lib (integrations and mobile copies differ) and `integrations/page.scss` copies the shared layout. Moving `bf-load-state` into `libs/web/ui` would let G11 be fixed once.
- **Positives worth keeping:** connector status tags always carry text + icon; money in tables goes through `bf-money` with `bf-num` on both `th` and `td` (pushes, tasks, webhooks), and no header/cell alignment mismatch was found at any viewport; no raw colours, no `font-family`, no undefined status tokens (S4) in these two libraries; Inter-Director Budget Contribution Request is spelled exactly.

## Area H — admin, org, finance-admin, extended

Scope: 103, 103a, 102b, 105, 111, 114, 010a, 122, 120a, 120b, 122a, 122b, 015c (all states in `screens.json`), plus the uncovered routes of `libs/web/features/{admin,org,finance-admin,extended}`. 107-exchange-rates, 108-fiscal-calendar and 022c-hr-headcount-import were captured ad hoc and compared with their Stitch exports.

Evidence (all under `tmp/audit/`):

- Viewport captures: `screens/`.
- Full-content captures, viewport grown to the scroll height: `extra/H/tall/<id>--<state>--<viewport>.png`.
- Ad hoc captures, probe, parity and capture report: `extra/H/` (`screens/`, `probe/`, `design-parity/`, `report.md`).
- Tablet and mobile probe: `extra/H/probe-vp/`.
- Probe summary: `H-probe-summary.txt`.
- Measurement scripts: `tools/{tall,cls,cls2,clip,over,sr,abs,dead}-H.mjs`.

Shared IDs (fork A writes these up in detail):

- **S1**: KPI card is not the section 9 anatomy.
- **S2**: Table is not the section 6 dense table.
- **S3**: Status-tag tone or icon differs from section 5.
- **S4**: Undefined `--sat-sys-{info,success,warning}` tokens.
- **S5**: Budget-bar colours.
- **S6**: Money or numbers formatted outside the Angular formatters or `bf-money`.
- **S7**: Page-title typography.
- **S8**: Card radius 16px instead of 12px.
- **S9**: Visual QA coverage.
- **S10**: Inline `font-family`.

Facts that hold on every screen below (measured, not repeated per row):

- **Page title (S7).** `h1` renders Figtree 24px weight 400.
- **Tables (S2).**
  - Header 56px (mat-table) or 37px (`bf-split-table`), rows 52–94px, against the spec's 52/48px.
  - Header 14px or 12px weight 500, not uppercase, on a white or transparent background instead of `surface-container-low`.
  - No `sat-avatar`, no sorting, no paginator.
- **Cards and KPIs (S8, S1).** `.bf-card` and `.bf-kpi` use `corner-large` (16px). KPI values are 24px weight 400, with no icon, footer or bar.
- **Alignment.** No header/cell alignment mismatch was found at 1440, 834 or 412 on any table in scope (probe `cols[].mismatch`).

### Spec conflicts / notes

- **Layout-shift root cause (105, 111, 108, 033).** `bf-load-state` paints five 20px `sat-ghost` boxes (`admin/load-state.ts:13-16`; each lib has its own `load-state.ts`). Pages load several independent resources, so lower cards paint first and are then pushed down when the first resource replaces its short ghost. Some pages also insert a banner above content after load (`retention.page.html:22-29`, the 108 warning banner).
  - Shared fix: let `LoadState` take `rows` and a `rowHeight` (default 48) plus a header ghost, so the placeholder matches the table it replaces.
  - Render page sections in order: `@if` lower sections on the primary resource's `hasValue()`.
  - Never insert content above already-painted content after load.
  - The 0.5–0.7 values in 105's search states also include shifts after the Playwright `fill` (programmatic fill does not set `hadRecentInput`). Real typing would be partly excused, but the 0.19–0.30 load shift is real (`tools/cls-H.mjs` output).
- **Visual QA never captures below the fold (cross-cutting, for the parent report).** Every capture is exactly the viewport height: 0 of 258 desktop captures are taller than 900px. The shell scrolls an inner container, so `fullPage: true` grows nothing. Baselines and the QA loop therefore miss everything below the first screen, for example 107's FX effect and rate history, and 111's legal holds and retention runs. Fix in `scripts/visual/capture.mjs` and `visual.spec.ts`: before the screenshot, find the main scroller and grow the viewport to its `scrollHeight` (as `tools/tall-H.mjs` does), or screenshot the scroller element with `locator.screenshot()` after unsetting its height in a test-only style tag.
- **Expected-failure states lack `allowRequests`** in `screens.json`: 016a, 061f, 087a and 102d are outside my scope but appear as blocking console errors in `report.md`.
- **Duplicated stylesheets.** `admin/`, `org/` and `finance-admin/` each keep a ~200-line copy of the shared `page-layout.scss` (with their own `.bf-kpi*`, `.bf-card` radius and `.bf-sr`). The copies have drifted: admin fixed the absolute `.bf-sr` that stretches the page, while org and finance-admin still ship it and overflow on mobile. `extended/outcomes.page.ts` and `organizer.page.ts` redefine KPI CSS a fourth and fifth time. Consolidate into `libs/web/ui/src/styles/page-layout.scss` so the S1, S2 and S8 fixes land once.
- **The `min-width: 680px` rule** for administration tables (`admin/page.scss:79`) is why every admin table is clipped by 300–370px at 412px. It prevents squashing but hides status and actions. The responsive fix is stacked rows below 600px (UX 103 and 108 both ask for read-only mobile lists), not a smaller minimum.
- **Satori vs sheet.** Satori `sat-banner` has no error variant, so the sheet's error-container hard block maps to `variant="important"`; that is correct as built. Satori's empty-state headline renders at about 28px, while the sheet's section 12 shows 16px bold with an icon circle; not fixable without overriding `.sat-*` internals (`no-ds-internals-overrides`), so the Satori rendering should govern. Headings render in Figtree (Satori), while the sheet uses Plus Jakarta Sans; Satori governs, so only weight differences are actionable.
- **Branding accent colour (102b).** A tenant-entered hex (`#1F4FD8`) is stored and shown. It is used in emails only (hint at `settings.page.html:84`); applying it in-app would break the tokens-only rule.
- **S4 in my scope:** only `admin-ui.ts:65`. **S10 in my scope:** only `figures.ts:303`.
- **Lock icon availability.** The Satori sprite already serves `svgIcon="lock"` (used at `wallets.page.ts:317`), so the lock-icon fixes for 107, 108 and 111 need no new icon set.

Ordered by blocking rows removed per unit of change, then by the number of screens resolved. Each fix lands once in `libs/web/ui` (or the harness) and is then adopted by the features. Do them in this order: fixes 1–3 restore budget-safety distinctions that are wrong today, and fix 12 makes visual QA able to prove the rest.
